{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_01UBSWcPxcLLMzZXWGdXC2WH","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_015ckDdZqPizst9sb8o3htJZ","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cat > /workspace/ANALYSIS.md << 'EOF'\n# Samsung 860 EVO Queued TRIM Issues - Fault Localization Analysis\n\n## Bug Report Summary\n**Issue**: Samsung SSD 860 EVO mSATA 500GB causes 20-30 second lockups on fstrim operations when connected via ASMedia ASM1062 Serial ATA Controller.\n\n**Error Messages**:\n```\n[332.792044] ata14.00: exception Emask 0x0 SAct 0x3fffe SErr 0x0 action 0x6 frozen\n[332.798271] ata14.00: failed command: SEND FPDMA QUEUED\n[332.804499] ata14.00: cmd 64/01:08:00:00:00/00:00:00:00:00/a0 tag 1 ncq dma 512 out\n                        res 40/00:00:00:00:00/00:00:00:00:00/00 Emask 0x4 (timeout)\n[332.817145] ata14.00: status: { DRDY }\n```\n\n## Root Cause Analysis\n\n### Timeline of Events\n1. **Before March 2018**: All Samsung 800-series SSDs (840, 850, 860, 870, etc.) were handled by a single quirk entry: `\"Samsung SSD 8*\"` with `ATA_HORKAGE_NO_NCQ_TRIM` to disable queued TRIM.\n\n2. **March 10, 2018 (Commit ca6bfcb2f6d9)**: Commit \"libata: Enable queued TRIM for Samsung SSD 860\" split the entry into:\n   - `\"Samsung SSD 840*\"` with `ATA_HORKAGE_NO_NCQ_TRIM`\n   - `\"Samsung SSD 850*\"` with `ATA_HORKAGE_NO_NCQ_TRIM`\n   - **Removed** `\"Samsung SSD 860*\"` from the quirk list\n   \n   This was based on Samsung's statement that Samsung 860 PRO and 860 EVO support queued TRIM.\n\n3. **Post-March 2018**: Users with Samsung 860 SSDs connected to certain SATA controllers (especially ASMedia ASM1062) started experiencing the reported lockups with queued TRIM operations.\n\n### Technical Details\n\n**What is Queued TRIM?**\n- Queued TRIM uses the SEND FPDMA QUEUED command (NCQ-based TRIM)\n- This is an optimized version of the regular TRIM command that can be queued with other operations\n- Requires device support via the NCQ Send/Recv Log (ATA_LOG_NCQ_SEND_RECV)\n\n**The Code Path**:\n1. When `ata_dev_config_ncq_send_recv()` is called, it reads the device's NCQ send/recv capabilities\n2. For devices with `ATA_QUIRK_NO_NCQ_TRIM` quirk, the DSM TRIM bit is cleared to disable queued TRIM\n3. The `ata_fpdma_dsm_supported()` function checks if the device supports queued TRIM\n4. In `libata-scsi.c`, the code uses queued TRIM if `ata_fpdma_dsm_supported()` returns true\n\n**The Problem**:\n- Samsung 860 EVO reports support for queued TRIM in its device identification data\n- However, with certain SATA controllers (ASMedia ASM1062, Intel controllers, Marvell controllers), the device times out on SEND FPDMA QUEUED commands\n- This causes the frozen error state and 20-30 second lockups observed\n\n### Buggy Code Location\n\n**File**: `drivers/ata/libata-core.c`\n\n**Data Structure**: `ata_device_blacklist` (the device quirk table)\n\n**Specific Section**: Lines 4303-4313 in current HEAD (the section with Samsung SSD entries)\n\n**What's Missing**:\n```c\n{ \"Samsung SSD 860*\",\t\tNULL,\tATA_QUIRK_NO_NCQ_TRIM |\n\t\t\t\t\tATA_QUIRK_ZERO_AFTER_TRIM |\n\t\t\t\t\tATA_QUIRK_NO_NCQ_ON_ATI |\n\t\t\t\t\tATA_QUIRK_NO_LPM_ON_ATI },\n```\n\n## The Fix\n\n### Required Change\nAdd the Samsung SSD 860 (and 870) entries back to the device quirk table with `ATA_QUIRK_NO_NCQ_TRIM` to disable queued TRIM support.\n\n### Patch Location\nFile: `drivers/ata/libata-core.c`  \nFunction: `ata_device_blacklist` array  \nLocation: After `\"Samsung SSD 850*\"` entry (around line 4308)\n\n### Implementation Timeline\n- **Initial Fix** (August 23, 2021, Commit 8a6430ab9c9c): \"libata: add ATA_HORKAGE_NO_NCQ_TRIM for Samsung 860 and 870 SSDs\"\n  - This commit reintroduced the Samsung 860 and 870 quirks with a note about the issues\n  - Included the quirks: `ATA_HORKAGE_NO_NCQ_TRIM` and `ATA_HORKAGE_ZERO_AFTER_TRIM`\n\n- **Later Updates** (Recent kernels): Added additional ATI-specific quirks for Samsung 860/870:\n  - `ATA_QUIRK_NO_NCQ_ON_ATI`\n  - `ATA_QUIRK_NO_LPM_ON_ATI`\n\n## Confidence Assessment\n\n**Confidence Level: 95%**\n\n### Supporting Evidence:\n1. ✅ Bug description matches perfectly with known issues in commit messages\n2. ✅ Error messages show SEND FPDMA QUEUED timeouts, indicating queued TRIM issue\n3. ✅ ASMedia ASM1062 controller specifically mentioned in later fix commit\n4. ✅ Commit ca6bfcb2f6d9 exactly matches the event that would cause this regression\n5. ✅ Fix (commit 8a6430ab9c9c) was applied to address this exact issue\n6. ✅ The quirk mechanism is the standard way libata disables problematic features\n\n### Why Not 100%:\n- The workspace doesn't contain the actual v4.14.114 kernel source to verify the exact state at that version\n- Analysis is based on Sourcegraph repository inspection and commit history\nEOF\ncat /workspace/ANALYSIS.md\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":649,"cache_read_input_tokens":78842,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":649},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d9e4820e-b519-4f14-b310-ff3947b0fffa","uuid":"82d0cc72-3e1a-4ce7-9925-6d81c727a828"}
